                              - text: This is the start of my guide for

                                - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/arc

                                - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/spell-totem
                              - generic [ref=f1227e316]: Gemling Legionnaire
                              - text: . The idea is to double-dip on the quality bonuses provided from
                              - generic [ref=f1227e321]: Gemling Legionnaire
                              - text: to skill gems since both
                              - link "Arc" [ref=f1227e327] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/arc
                              - text: and
                              - link "Spell Totem" [ref=f1227e332] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/spell-totem
                              - text: have INSANE bonuses granting 6% more damage per remaining chain ( about 30 chains currently or 180% more damage) and 120% cast speed with 6 totems up. For all stages of the guide try to have your
                              - link "Spell Totem" [ref=f1227e337] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/spell-totem
                              - text: around level 23, this is the minimum level for +3 totem limit while costing the least amount of mana. You can give a couple additional levels for additional totem tankiness but keep this in mind.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1227e341]:
                              - text: READ
                              - generic [ref=f1227e342]: Ancestral Bond
                              - text: . This makes it so our totems cost spirit instead of power/endurance charges.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1227e347]:
                              - strong [ref=f1227e348]: Setups are available for all budget levels, this build does NOT require a high budget in any way to completely crush all content in the game.
                            - paragraph [ref=f1227e349]:
                              - strong [ref=f1227e350]: LIVE RIGHT NOW
                              - text: "-"

                                  - 'tab "Act 1 {1}" [selected] [ref=f1227e399] [cursor=pointer]'
                                  - 'tab "Act 2 {2}" [ref=f1227e402] [cursor=pointer]'

                                  - 'tab "Act 4 {4}" [ref=f1227e406] [cursor=pointer]'
                                  - 'tab "Interludes+ {5}" [ref=f1227e408] [cursor=pointer]'
                                  - tab "Leaguestart Absolute Minimum Entry (lvl 70 ish)" [ref=f1227e410] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "Leaguestart Absolute Minimum Entry Step 2" [ref=f1227e412] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "SUPER LOW BUDGET ( Warrior Variant Jewel )" [ref=f1227e414] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "Low Budget (Templar Jewel)" [ref=f1227e416] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "Medium Budget" [ref=f1227e418] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "Medium Budget Step 2" [ref=f1227e420] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "Besos-Maxing Step 1 [ ~500-600 Div ish ]" [ref=f1227e422] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "Besos-Maxing Step 2 [ 1100 Div ish ]" [ref=f1227e424] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "Besos-Maxing Step 3 [ ~1800div ish ]" [ref=f1227e426] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "Elon-Maxing Step 1 [ 3300div idk ]" [ref=f1227e428] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "The Pinnacle [ Mirror-Tier ]" [ref=f1227e430] [cursor=pointer]
                                  - tab "Acts Twink Spark Leveling Setup" [ref=f1227e432] [cursor=pointer]
                              - generic [ref=f1227e435]:
                                - button "Previous variant" [disabled] [ref=f1227e436]
                                - button "Next variant" [ref=f1227e438] [cursor=pointer]
                            - generic [ref=f1227e446]:
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e447]:
                                - text: Starter Video -
                                - link "Youtube" [ref=f1227e448] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://youtu.be/t6HXjq8Wnsg
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e449]:
                                - text: Stream -
                                - link "Twitch" [ref=f1227e450] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://twitch.tv/pathofky
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e451]:
                                - text: Unfortunately you cannot level as
                                - link "Arc" [ref=f1227e453] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/arc
                                - link "Spell Totem" [ref=f1227e458] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/spell-totem
                                - text: as it requires a lot of passive points to make it to the
                                - generic [ref=f1227e462]: Ancestral Bond
                                - text: keystone which this build requires to function.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e467]: Instead we play as a Grenade Mercenary with the intent to swap to arc totems around level 65-70ish. There is unfortunately no way around this. You can play any leveling build you would like, but I have the setup I will be using laid out here.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e468]:
                                - text: Here you will use
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1227e470] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - link "Flash Grenade" [ref=f1227e475] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/flash-grenade
                                - link "Gas Grenade" [ref=f1227e480] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/gas-grenade
                                - link "Oil Grenade" [ref=f1227e485] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/oil-grenade
                                - link "Cluster Grenade" [ref=f1227e490] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/cluster-grenade
                                - text: ", to speedrun through campaign."
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e494]:
                                - text: "Leveling POB:"
                                - link "https://pobb.in/DttTF88yOjkA" [ref=f1227e495] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://pobb.in/DttTF88yOjkA
                              - heading "How to Play:" [level=3] [ref=f1227e496]
                              - heading "Act 1" [level=4] [ref=f1227e497]
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e498]:
                                - text: Spend your first support gem on
                                - link "Multishot I" [ref=f1227e500] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/multishot-i
                                - text: for your
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1227e505] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - text: followed by
                                - link "Multishot I" [ref=f1227e510] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/multishot-i
                                - text: for
                                - link "Flash Grenade" [ref=f1227e515] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/flash-grenade
                                - text: . You should fire your
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1227e520] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - text: far ahead of yourself and aim for the mobs to run into them as you are running away. This can take some getting used to.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e524]:
                                - text: For general clear you will be using primarily
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1227e526] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - text: ", cast it the moment you see a pack far ahead of you. This will be a long delay for now but it gets shorter and shorter as we progress."
                                - link "Flash Grenade" [ref=f1227e531] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/flash-grenade
                                - text: can be used for a more instant but less potent burst of damage. This also causes huge stun-buildup so you should use it on cd for bosses to build stun bar.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e535]:
                                - text: Try to cut a level 5 skill gem before the final boss for
                                - link "Gas Grenade" [ref=f1227e537] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/gas-grenade
                                - text: . Your priority should loosely be
                                - link "Frost Bomb" [ref=f1227e542] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/frost-bomb
                                - text: ( for exposure as needed ) >
                                - link "Explosive Grenade" [ref=f1227e547] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/explosive-grenade
                                - text: ">"
                                - link "Gas Grenade" [ref=f1227e552] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/gas-grenade
                                - text: ">"
                                - link "Flash Grenade" [ref=f1227e557] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/flash-grenade
                                - text: ( for stun buildup. ) As long as the mob is ingited
                                - link "Gas Grenade" [ref=f1227e562] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: /poe-2/database/gems/gas-grenade
                                - text: will pop instantly.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e566]:
                                - strong [ref=f1227e567]: "Focus on +# to # added X damage on your crossbow and both rings. It is INCREDIBLY important to keep this value as high as possible as it is the baseline for all your skills damage."
                                - text: Check Vendors constantly to keep your weapon and rings up to date.
                              - paragraph [ref=f1227e568]: For Armour Pieces look to use Either Armour or Armour/Evasion Hyrbid Pieces with:. +# to Maximum Life. +#% Increased Cold/Fire/Lightning Resistnace. High Total Aarmour/Evasion Rating Value
                          - generic [ref=f1227e571]:

                        - generic [ref=f1227e1265]:
                          - heading "How it Plays" [level=2] [ref=f1227e1269]
                          - generic [ref=f1227e1276]:
                            - heading "FAQ" [level=3] [ref=f1227e1277]
                            - paragraph [ref=f1227e1278]:
                              - text: Its VERY important for single target that you have your weapon set passives set correctly in both your passive tree and gem assignments.
                              - strong [ref=f1227e1279]: Also MAKE SURE your last cast is either a totem or mana tempest so that you are resting in set 1 ( archmage ).
                            - heading "When do I swap?" [level=4] [ref=f1227e1280]
                            - paragraph [ref=f1227e1281]: Swap when you have a gear set prepared to put you around 3.5k mana and you are AT MINIMUM level 70. BOTH OF THESE TOGETHER ARE REQUIRED not 1 or the other. Mana regen on rings and spirit on both your chest and amulet are very important aswell.
                            - heading "Upgrade order?" [level=4] [ref=f1227e1282]
                            - paragraph [ref=f1227e1283]: Buy Low Basic Stater Set of Gears -> !gring, !wand -> Buy/Semi-Craft a Cheap Medium Budget Amulet / Scepter / Helm / Chest / Boots / Gloves !gamulet -> !gbody -> !ghelm -> !ggloves -> !fork -> Mageblood -> !ggbody -> !gsceptre
